# Module map

Generated by scripts/index/generate-module-map.py from Package.swift and the Sources tree. Do not edit by hand.
Regenerate with `python3 scripts/index/generate-module-map.py` and check currency with `python3 scripts/checks/module-map-current.py`.

The map holds no file counts, no line counts and no line numbers, so a lane regenerates it only when it adds, removes or renames a target, a public type or a folder. A target that holds C files lists them in its own section. Public types are top-level `public` or `open` declarations found at column 0.
